- **L127** (2026-10-30, shaper): T12 shaped. S15 rewritten with acceptance criteria (a)-(i); T12 split into T12a (removed flags in T1, `Risk: none`), T12b (the T4 check and its record, with the leftovers, `Risk: 1`), T12c (orphaned fixtures, `Risk: none`), T12d (dead dependencies through `go mod tidy -diff` in T2, `Risk: 5`), T12e (the read-only verb `review proposals` for merged branches and stale worktrees, `Risk: none`) and T12f (the sweeper's role line and skill, `Risk: 3`), each inline, sequential, inside `own-rdd-t12`. No command was run (the shaper has no shell); every claim below is from a file read, or marked.
  - Code read: `engine/review/app/sweep.go` (whole), `engine/review/sweep.go` (whole), `engine/review/rolerecords.go:1-258`, `engine/review/app/app.go:76-179`, `engine/review/gitexec/gitexec.go:398-698`, `:860-905`, `engine/review/analyzers/analyzers.go` (whole), `engine/cmd/review_sweep.go` (whole), `engine/cmd/usage.go:215-223`, `engine/cmd/review.go:62,86`, `engine/cmd/review_sweep_test.go:18-52,281-294`, `engine/cmd/live_guard_test.go` (test names), `engine/skills/role_chain_artifact_test.go:80-119`, `README.md:722-737`, `bin/lib/usage.sh:306-319`, `.gitignore`, `roles/sweeper/body.md`, `skills/sweeper/SKILL.md` (the sweeper trigger left `role-chain` for it in T17c, L91), `docs/architecture/role-design/per-role-sweeper.md`, and the review result `a2186905faf2-1.json` (location only). Log entries read: L30 (`go mod tidy -diff` exists locally and fails offline on a module missing from the cache), L44, L57 (the guard's `Spec:` path, carried), L115 (the form of this design), L126.
  - Steelman of S15 as designed: the sweep is the one place that already runs once per commit, hands the sweeper a mechanical candidate list and is held by `review check`, the stop hook and CI (S6, S7); putting duties 1-4 there makes "the project always clean" a property each commit is checked for (S1), not a habit, and keeps the sweeper the removal-only agent of `per-role-sweeper.md` (tools find, the agent judges, false positives are recorded `live`). Duties that are not of a commit (branches, worktrees) stay outside the record as proposals, so nothing outside the tree is ever removed without the owner.
  - Strongest counter-argument: every new check makes more commits fire, and each fired sweep costs a sweeper launch and a record before delivery; two of the checks have known false positives (a CHANGELOG line naming a removed flag, a fixture read through a glob), and the tidy check adds a dependency on `go` and on the module cache that L30 saw fail on this machine, so S1 turns an offline machine into a stopped delivery for any commit touching a module's dependencies. Accepted tradeoff: every check reports only what the commit itself adds, the false positives are cheap `live` answers that the sweeper records with their proof, and the tidy check runs only for commits that can make a dependency dead (D33). Rejected: a repository-wide "sweep the project" verb (it would find old debt, which `per-role-sweeper.md:41` assigns to a builder unit); the sweeper agent running its own greps (prose, not a check, S1); folding leftovers and fixtures into T2 entries (no format change, but T2 is the analyzers' new lines; D37).
  - Open decisions (owner): **D31** removed verbs and other strings are not searched by T1, only Go and shell names and flags: recommended, since a removed verb's handler (`run<Verb>`) and its flags are already found; tradeoff, a README sentence that names a removed verb by its spoken name (`review foo`) is not a candidate. **D32** the leftover suffixes are exactly `.orig` and `.bak`, the two the owner named: recommended; tradeoff, `.rej`, `.tmp` or `.old` are not found (`*.swp` and `*~` are ignored by `.gitignore` and never committed), and scratch scripts are found only untracked, through T3. **D33** the tidy check: (1) it runs only for a module whose `go.mod` or `go.sum` the commit changes or whose `.go` files lose a non-standard import; (2) it runs under the environment the analyzers get, with no `GOFLAGS` or `GOPROXY` forced; (3) a tidy that cannot run refuses the sweep (S1). Recommended all three; tradeoff, on a machine whose module cache lacks a module (L30) such a commit cannot be swept or delivered until `go mod download` runs; the alternative, a tidy failure recorded as a finding, passes a check that did not run (against S1). **D34** who removes a dead requirement: the sweeper, in its `chore(sweep)` commit with `Risk: 5` (a dependency change, Task Size item 5), so that commit then needs its polisher records and its own review: recommended (it is the duty the owner accepted, and the chain already holds such a commit to every check); alternative, the sweeper answers `finding` and the parent removes it in a commit of its own (line 9 of the role would then also name requirements). **D35** proposals: a separate read-only verb `review proposals` that the parent runs after a pull request merges (the sweeper skill line of T12f), relays each proposal to the owner and runs a proposed command only after the owner confirms that one; local branches only (deleting a remote branch is publishing); the commands are git's refusing forms (`git worktree remove`, `git branch -d`), never `--force` or `-D`; the verb name is an assumption. Recommended; tradeoff, a squash- or rebase-merged branch is never proposed, and a worktree with uncommitted work is proposed when its head merged (git refuses to remove it, which is the safety). Whether the parent may ever run a proposed command without a per-proposal confirmation is not designed: S15 says the owner approves. **D36** the orphaned-fixture rule (no `.go` file directly in the fixture's package names the fixture or a directory above it, new at the head): recommended; tradeoff, a fixture read through a glob or from another package is a false positive answered `live`, and a fixture whose name appears in any `.go` file of its package (even a comment) is never found. **D37** the record gains a member `t4`, written always and read as empty when absent: recommended (a named check, as T1-T3); tradeoff, a persisted format change (T12b `Risk: 1`): every stored record keeps decoding (pinned by a test over every `*.sweep.json` of the checkout), but a `labdrian` older than T12b refuses the new records, so the installed binary is upgraded with the merge; alternative, leftovers and fixtures as T2 entries (no format change, a less honest name).
  - Not shaped, carried to T12 by earlier entries and outside S15 (the owner's words name neither): SUGGESTION F1 of `a2186905faf2-1.json` (`phase8SameEntries` sorts the hook lists, so the repair assertion no longer checks order; carried in L44) and the builder guard resolving `Spec:` against the parent's working directory (L57). Owner rule: every finding is resolved; the parent decides whether they become a task of their own (recommended) before T12 closes.

The role file `roles/sweeper/body.md` changes (line 8) and is regenerated for the three runtimes.
